# Skills · H · part 16

886 entries, honey-memory to horizon-scanning-trace. [Index](README.md) · [Everything](../README.md)

- **honey-memory**: Create and maintain one committed per-project memory file (PROJECT.md) so agents stop re-discovering the same facts every cold session. Stores only the stable, expensive-to-rediscover, not-in-the-code context — architecture + why, build/test/run commands, decisions and dead-ends — as human-readable markdown, versioned with the code so it can't silently rot. Use when asked to set up project memory/context, stop agents re-grepping the codebase, scaffold or refresh a PROJECT.md / CLAUDE.md memory section, or cut cold-start rediscovery cost. Prose context only — never code, config, or data. ([Green-PT/honey-for-devs](https://github.com/Green-PT/honey-for-devs/tree/HEAD/plugins/honey/skills/honey-memory) · ★308 · 2 copies)
- **honey-px**: Read huge read-only text as PNG pages; big input cut. ([Green-PT/honey-for-devs](https://github.com/Green-PT/honey-for-devs/tree/HEAD/.hermes/skills/honey-px) · ★308)
- **honey-px**: Read huge dense read-only text as PNG pages via pxpipe export: ~60-75% fewer input tokens. Lossy on exact strings; never for files you will edit. ([Green-PT/honey-for-devs](https://github.com/Green-PT/honey-for-devs/tree/HEAD/.openclaw/skills/honey-px) · ★308)
- **honey-px**: Read huge, dense, read-only text as rendered PNG pages instead of raw text — image tokens scale with pixels, not characters, so token-dense bulk (big files, vendored code, diffs, logs) costs ~60–75% less as an image. Use when you must skim or reason over thousands of lines you will NOT edit or byte-copy. Lossy on exact strings: never for files you'll Edit, secrets, hashes, or byte-exact values. Fable-class readers only. ([Green-PT/honey-for-devs](https://github.com/Green-PT/honey-for-devs/tree/HEAD/plugins/honey/skills/honey-px) · ★308 · 2 copies)
- **honey-review**: Review a diff for over-engineering and verbosity. Terse delete-list of what to cut and the lines each saves. One-shot. ([Green-PT/honey-for-devs](https://github.com/Green-PT/honey-for-devs/tree/HEAD/.openclaw/skills/honey-review) · ★308)
- **honey-review**: Review a diff for what Honey would cut — over-engineering (speculative generality, hand-rolled stdlib, single-caller abstractions) and over-verbosity (dead code, narration, redundant comments). Outputs a terse delete-list, one line per finding, with the line count each cut saves. Use when asked to review, trim, or de-bloat a change, or before opening a PR. Quality lever only — it finds waste, not correctness bugs. ([Green-PT/honey-for-devs](https://github.com/Green-PT/honey-for-devs/tree/HEAD/plugins/honey/skills/honey-review) · ★308 · 2 copies)
- **honey-review**: Review a diff for over-engineering; terse delete-list. ([Green-PT/honey-for-devs](https://github.com/Green-PT/honey-for-devs/tree/HEAD/.hermes/skills/honey-review) · ★308)
- **honey-superpowers**: Inject Honey levers into dispatched subagent prompts. ([Green-PT/honey-for-devs](https://github.com/Green-PT/honey-for-devs/tree/HEAD/.hermes/skills/honey-superpowers) · ★308)
- **honey-superpowers**: Stack Honey onto Superpowers-style workflows (subagent-driven-development, dispatching-parallel-agents, executing-plans) and any orchestration that dispatches fresh subagents. Dispatched subagents run in isolated context and do NOT inherit the session's Honey hook, so they emit full-fat code and verbose reports. Use when you are about to dispatch implementer/reviewer/fixer/parallel subagents: it gives the exact Honey directive to paste into each dispatch prompt, with a worker variant and a reviewer variant, so the levers apply inside the subagents too — where the multiplied cost actually is. ([Green-PT/honey-for-devs](https://github.com/Green-PT/honey-for-devs/tree/HEAD/plugins/honey/skills/honey-superpowers) · ★308 · 2 copies)

- **honeycomb-metrics-queries**: Load before querying a Honeycomb OpenTelemetry metrics dataset (dataset_type=metrics): gauges, counters, histograms and sums, including infrastructure metrics such as CPU, memory, disk I/O and network. Metrics follow different rules from trace datasets: bare COUNT, RATE_SUM, RATE_AVG, RATE_MAX and CONCURRENCY are rejected, temporal aggregation is automatic, and each metric has its own attributes. Not for event datasets that only have metrics in their name. ([aws/tools-for-devops-agent](https://github.com/aws/tools-for-devops-agent/tree/HEAD/skills/honeycomb-metrics-queries) · ★68)

- **honorarios-da-fazenda**: Honorários nas causas em que a Fazenda é parte, pelo CPC 85 verbatim: as cinco faixas do §3º (10-20% até 200 SM · 8-10% até 2.000 · 5-8% até 20.000 · 3-5% até 100.000 · 1-3% acima), a aplicação escalonada do §5º — faixa inicial e, no que exceder, a subsequente, como imposto de renda, nunca percentual único sobre o todo —, o §4º (líquida aplica desde logo, ilíquida só na liquidação), o §6º (vale inclusive na improcedência) e o §6º-A (é PROIBIDA a apreciação equitativa quando o valor é líquido ou liquidável, salvo o §8º).
